# macOS action inventory for OPT-1867

This is the **candidate register**, not the completed parity verdict. It enumerates every variant in the reference `UiAction` family declarations. A declared action may be compatibility-only or lack an active macOS control; direct hotkey routes are identified where present, while runtime availability and the separate visible-control inventory remain open. A reference action-catalog fixture is evidence of the reference contract only. A source-level classification does not establish native GPUI parity where a row records pending manual evidence.

- Reference source: [`yield-legacy` `c80002becec0071298fa110ee0935edee9957978`](https://github.com/PORTALSURFER/yield-legacy/tree/c80002becec0071298fa110ee0935edee9957978/src/app_core/actions/ui_projection_actions).
- Active GPUI source: [`yield` `144cfaf329c66482e6d0640bc24828a9f08e3c8f`](https://github.com/PORTALSURFER/yield/tree/144cfaf329c66482e6d0640bc24828a9f08e3c8f).
- Current review baseline for the sample-row slice: [`yield` `97c8c2795e7b369434595a2bbbcddfd60ed8a57e`](https://github.com/PORTALSURFER/yield/tree/97c8c2795e7b369434595a2bbbcddfd60ed8a57e). Earlier candidate rows retain their stated baseline until individually refreshed.
- Current review baseline for the shell folder slice: [`yield` `2a1f334a162a9a89106477957916ca6166cdcbef`](https://github.com/PORTALSURFER/yield/tree/2a1f334a162a9a89106477957916ca6166cdcbef). The reference native folder buttons send `FolderBrowserMessage` variants, so declared shell actions are not treated as extra active controls without a direct route.
- Waveform action source-level review uses [`yield` `b62df23cd340df6d34d6a1413c5ad0385b75dcef`](https://github.com/PORTALSURFER/yield/tree/b62df23cd340df6d34d6a1413c5ad0385b75dcef) and the reference commit above. It distinguishes declared controller actions from active native `WaveformInteraction` routes; the current document edits are not native test results.
- The source-row action slice also uses GPUI `2a1f334a162a9a89106477957916ca6166cdcbef`. The reference native source row and context menu dispatch by source ID, while several declared `SourcesFolders` variants address an index or focused source; similar labels alone do not establish identical targeting.
- The active native shortcut and workspace-layout sweep uses reference `c80002becec0071298fa110ee0935edee9957978` and GPUI `493bd34c742c0ed58c28645a7d554504668aae1a`. Later inventory-only commits do not alter the GPUI handlers cited in those rows.
- The 2026-09-26 review refresh below uses the tested PR heads [#88 `66e242878`](https://github.com/PORTALSURFER/yield/pull/88) (X shortcut), [#89 `35537f50e`](https://github.com/PORTALSURFER/yield/pull/89) (workspace dividers), [#92 `085d9c168`](https://github.com/PORTALSURFER/yield/pull/92) (browser columns), [#93 `5d096d154`](https://github.com/PORTALSURFER/yield/pull/93) (worker details), and [#95 `c6c6f9a2e`](https://github.com/PORTALSURFER/yield/pull/95) (Settings focus). PRs #88, #89, and #93 are merged into `origin/main` `b6444640ea79df95b6fe7664d260253c43957879`; #92 and #95 remain separate. Each native result belongs only to the named isolated build and does not update the older baseline of every other row. OPT-1910 remains To Review because native full-zoom selection/focus acceptance is incomplete despite its merge.
- `OPT-1474` retained macOS staged publication and cleanup as unsupported. Reference actions requiring physical publication or cleanup remain unmet until `OPT-1868` qualifies a production primitive; a source-list unregistration is a different operation. Neither a projected control nor a headless fixture waives the physical-operation gate.
- Per-family tickets below are triage owners, **not** proof that they cover every individual action. Each row with pending native evidence still needs a concrete comparison or an explicit unreachable-action decision before this inventory can pass.

### Waveform action review at GPUI `b62df23cd340df6d34d6a1413c5ad0385b75d52f5`

All 205 declared action rows now have a source-level classification, including the Waveform family. This is a routing inventory, not a native parity verdict. The active reference waveform widget sends `WaveformInteraction` messages for selection, fade, viewport, sample slide, and region export; their similarly named controller actions are not necessarily the widget's dispatch path. Four `BeginWaveform*Shift*` variants are runtime-internal catalog entries whose scenario dispatch is explicitly rejected. The GPUI mark-move gesture is a candidate for the active move interaction, not evidence that those four enum variants are user-facing controls.

The active reference Command-Option sample-audio slide has no GPUI waveform route identified; its prepared edit and publication work belongs to OPT-1475 and OPT-1868. External selection-region dragging belongs to OPT-1478 with macOS receiver evidence in OPT-1626. Both remain open and must not be satisfied by GPUI's ordinary play/edit mark drag or whole-file drag. Selection, fade, pan, and zoom rows identify GPUI route candidates and nearby tests, with native comparison still pending under OPT-1492. The separate visible-control pass still needs remaining app surfaces and exact native evidence.

## Active native shortcut pass: playback and selection

The reference [shortcut help entries](https://github.com/PORTALSURFER/yield-legacy/blob/c80002becec0071298fa110ee0935edee9957978/src/native_app/shell/shortcuts/help/defaults.rs#L29) are checked against the active [native bindings](https://github.com/PORTALSURFER/yield-legacy/blob/c80002becec0071298fa110ee0935edee9957978/src/native_app/shell/shortcuts.rs#L270), not just the declared `UiAction` catalog. GPUI's [key dispatcher](../crates/yield-ui/src/browser/mod.rs#L2552) and [shortcut-help rows](../crates/yield-ui/src/browser/shortcut_help.rs#L226) are separate candidate surfaces. The reference uses state-dependent bindings; a matching key label alone is insufficient.

## Smallest currently implementable leaves from this pass

These source-confirmed GPUI routes have existing building blocks and no new backend ownership decision in their bounded acceptance. Work through one issue at a time while keeping the next in Backlog. Native evidence remains part of each leaf's own acceptance.

1. [OPT-1909](https://linear.app/boostnlvp/issue/OPT-1909/restore-control-space-and-option-space-random-section-audition): random-section Space chords have draft PR #87 and app-targeted native transport evidence; physical-key OS interception and human audible acceptance remain open.
2. [OPT-1910](https://linear.app/boostnlvp/issue/OPT-1910/match-zoom-state-x-shortcut-behavior-in-yield-gpui): X state precedence is merged in PR #88, but the full-zoom native selection/focus branch remains To Review.
3. [OPT-1911](https://linear.app/boostnlvp/issue/OPT-1911/restore-yield-workspace-sidebar-and-waveform-panel-resizing): workspace dividers are merged and Done in PR #89. Inner filter/collection and browser-column layout remain separate.

The macOS-wide audit links destructive and normalization omissions to [OPT-1734](https://linear.app/boostnlvp/issue/OPT-1734/1487-c-inventory-remaining-active-reference-actions-decision), which remains Backlog with its own prerequisites. No destructive edit, extraction, file move, or cleanup row passes physical-publication acceptance until OPT-1868 qualifies it.

2. Reconcile each source-classified action/control with its active native route, GPUI candidate or confirmed gap, focused automated evidence, actual native/manual result or explicit absence, and bounded owner ticket. Zero `Unverified` action rows means the source classification pass is complete; it does not mean native parity passed.
3. Reconcile the `OPT-1734` destructive-action decision without duplicating its ownership; retain `OPT-1868` as unmet for physical publication and cleanup.
4. Record the next source and GPUI SHAs after changes, then derive the smallest implementable leaves from the completed table.
